Though the trading supply chain has been affected by the global economic           儘管全球經濟及地緣政治動盪波及貿易供應鏈，

which it operates, and its goals for each industry remain clear.

1.   BASIS OF PREPARATION                                                          1.    編製基礎
     The condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared                  簡明綜合財務報表乃根據香港會計師公會
     in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard (“HKAS”) 34 Interim                頒佈之香港會計準則第 34 號中期財務報告
     Financial Reporting issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public           以及香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規
     Accountants (the “HKICPA”) as well as with the applicable disclosure                則附錄 D2 之適用披露規定而編製。
     requirements of Appendix D2 to the Rules Governing the Listing of
     Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited.

     The preparation of the condensed consolidated financial statements                  編製簡明綜合財務報表需要管理層作出估
     requires management to make estimates and assumptions that affect                   計及假設，而該等估計及假設可影響於報
     the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and, disclosure of                   告期末所呈報之資產與負債金額及所披露
     contingent liabilities at the end of the reporting period and the reported          之或然負債，以及報告期內所呈報之收支
     amount of revenue and expenses during the reporting period.                         金額。



     The Group’s operations are seasonal. The revenue from September to                  本集團業務受季節因素影響，消費類電子
     January (the peak season for sales of consumer electronic products in               產品於中國大陸之銷售旺季為每年 9 月至
     the mainland China) is relatively higher than the revenue from the rest of          次年 1 月，此段期間之營業額相對高於其
     the year. Results for interim periods are not necessarily indicative of the         他月份之營業額，故中期業績未必反映整
     results for the entire financial year. This interim report should be read,          個財政年度之業績。此中期報告應與本集
     where relevant, in conjunction with the annual report of the Group for              團截至 2023 年 12 月 31 日止年度之年報（倘
     the year ended 31 December 2023.                                                    相關）一併閱讀。

2.   PRINCIPAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                 2.    主要會計政策
     The condensed consolidated financial statements have been prepared                  簡明綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基礎編
     on the historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments,              製，惟若干金融工具按公允值計量（如適
     which are measured at fair values, as appropriate.                                  用）除外。

     Other than additional accounting policies resulting from application of             除應用經修訂香港財務報告準則引致之額
     amendments to Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”),                   外 會 計 政 策 外，截 至 2024 年 6 月 30 日 止 六
     the accounting policies and methods of computation used in the                      個月之簡明綜合財務報表所採用之會計政
     condensed consolidated financial statements for the six months ended                策與計算方法，與本集團截至 2023 年 12 月
     30 June 2024 are the same as those presented in the Group’s annual                  31 日止年度之年度財務報表所呈列者相同。
     financial statements for the year ended 31 December 2023.

4.   SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                        4.    分部資料
     The Group is organised into operating business units according to the            本集團乃根據貨品銷售或提供服務之性質
     nature of the goods sold or services provided. The Group determines its          分類出營運業務單位。因此，本集團根據
     operating segments based on these business units by reference to the             業務單位所銷售之貨品或所提供之服務決
     goods sold or services provided, for the purpose of reporting to the             定其營運分部，以便向主要經營決策者（即
     chief operating decision maker (“CODM”) (i.e. the executive directors of         本公司之執行董事）呈報。提供獨立財務
     the Company). Individual operating segments for which discrete                   資料的個別經營分部由主要經營決策者鑒
     financial information is available are identified by the CODM and are            定並由其各自的管理團隊經營。該等個別
     operated by their respective management teams. These individual                  經營分部合計總額達致本集團可呈報分部。
     operating segments are aggregated in arriving at the reportable
     segments of the Group.

     Specifically, the Group’s reportable segments under HKFRS 8 Operating            具體而言，本集團於本中期期間根據香港
     Segments in the current interim period are as follows:                           財務報告準則第 8 號營運分部 確定之呈報
                                                                                      分部如下：

     1.      Smart Household Appliances            –   manufacture and sale of smart TV systems, smart white appliances and other
             Business                                  smart appliances such as smart air conditioners, smart refrigerators, smart
             智能家電業務                                    washing machines, smart kitchen appliances in the People’s Republic of China
                                                       (the “PRC”) and overseas markets, intelligent manufacturing, provision and sales
                                                       of internet value-added services of Coocaa system in the PRC, among others
                                                       中華人民共和國（「中國」）及海外市場之智能電視系統、智能白家電
                                                       產品及其他智能電器（例如智能空調、智能冰箱、智能洗衣機、智能
                                                       廚房電器等）之製造及銷售、中國之智能製造，以及提供及銷售酷開
                                                       系統的互聯網增值服務等

     Each of the operating segments under smart household appliances,                 智能家電、智能系統技術及新能源業務之
     smart systems technology and new energy businesses include                       各營運分部包括各自業務下之製造及╱或

     respective businesses. Each of these operations is considered as a               策 者 視 為 獨 立 經 營 分 部。就 分 部 報 告 而
     separate operating segment by the CODM. For the purpose of segment               言，此等個別經營分部已結集為可呈報分

     into reportable segments as set out above in order to present a more             本公司董事認為，提供各經營分部之詳情
     systematic and structured segment information. To give details of each           將導致篇幅過於冗長。
     of the operating segments, in the opinion of the directors of the

     Business and Others”.

     segment. No analysis of segment asset and segment liability is                           出決定。並無呈報分部資產及分部負債分
     presented as the CODM does not review such information for the                           析，因為主要經營決策者不就資源分配及

     Therefore, only segment revenue and segment results are presented.                       部收入及分部業績。

16. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF FINANCIAL                                           16. 金融工具之公允值計量
    INSTRUMENTS
     (a)   Fair value of the Group’s financial assets and financial                  (a)   按經常性基準以公允值計量本
           liabilities that are measured at fair value on a                                集團的金融資產及金融負債的
           recurring basis                                                                 公允值
           Some of the Group’s financial assets and financial liabilities are              本集團部分金融資產及金融負債於
           measured at fair value at the end of each reporting period. The                 每 個 報 告 期 末 均 以 公 允 值 計 量。以
           following table gives information about how the fair values of                  下表格提供如何釐定該等金融資產
           these financial assets and financial liabilities are determined (in             及金融負債之公允值的資料（特別是
           particular, the valuation technique(s) and inputs used), as well as             所採用的估值技術和輸入數據），以
           the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the fair value                 及公允值層級中根據輸入數據可觀
           measurements are categorised (levels 1 to 3) based on the degree                察程度分類的公允值計量等級（級別
           to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is observable.               1 至 3）。

           •     Level 1 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •    級別1公允值計量是來自活躍
                 quoted prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical                      市場對同類資產或負債的報價
                 assets or liabilities;                                                        （未經調整）；

           •     Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •   級別2公允值計量是來自撇除
                 inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1                       級別 1 內該資產或負債直接（等
                 that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly               同 價 格）或 間 接（源 自 價 格）可
                 (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices); and                觀察報價外之輸入數據；及

           •     Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from                    •   級別3公允值計量是來自估值
                 valuation techniques that include inputs for the asset or                     技術，包含並非基於可觀察市
                 liability that are not based on observable market data                        場數據的資產或負債之輸入數
                 (unobservable inputs).                                                        據（不可觀察輸入數據）。

                   equity securities with restriction for sale at FVTPL, and vice versa. A 5% (31                   按公允值計入損益的具出售限制之上市

                   (31 December 2023: RMB4 million).                                                                量保持不變，將導致按公允值計入損益

          (d)      Discount for lack of marketability of 11% (31 December 2023: 12%) is the key               (d)   缺乏市場流通性的貼現率 11%（2023 年 12

INTRODUCTION                                                                         引言
We have reviewed the condensed consolidated financial statements of                  本核數師已審閱第 27 至 65 頁所載的簡明綜合財
Skyworth Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively            務報表，包括創維集團有限公司（「貴公司」）及
referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 27 to 65, which comprise the            其 附 屬 公 司（統 稱「貴 集 團」）於 2024 年 6 月 30 日
condensed consolidated statement of financial position as of 30 June 2024 and        的簡明綜合財務狀況表，以及截至該日止六個
the related condensed consolidated statement of profit or loss and other             月期間的相關簡明綜合損益及其他全面收入表、
comprehensive income, statement of changes in equity and statement of cash           權益變動表及現金流量表及若干附註解釋。根
flows for the six-month period then ended, and certain explanatory notes. The        據香港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則規定，
Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong         中期財務資料報告須按照其相關條文以及香港
Limited require the preparation of a report on interim financial information to      會 計 師 公 會 頒 佈 的 香 港 會 計 準 則 第 34 號「中 期
be in compliance with the relevant provisions thereof and Hong Kong                  財務報告」   （「香港會計準則第 34 號」）編製。 貴
Accounting Standard 34 “Interim Financial Reporting” (“HKAS 34”) issued by the       公司董事負責按照香港會計準則第 34 號編製及
Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants. The directors of the            呈報該等簡明綜合財務報表。本核數師之責任
Company are responsible for the preparation and presentation of these                在於根據受聘之協定條款審閱該等簡明綜合財
condensed consolidated financial statements in accordance with HKAS 34. Our          務報表，就此達成結論，並僅向 閣下（作為整
responsibility is to express a conclusion on these condensed consolidated            體）匯報，而不作任何其他用途。本核數師不就
financial statements based on our review, and to report our conclusion solely        本報告之內容向任何其他人士承擔或負上任何
to you, as a body, in accordance with our agreed terms of engagement, and for        責任。
no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility towards or accept liability
to any other person for the contents of this report.

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                                      審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with Hong Kong Standard on Review              本核數師根據香港會計師公會頒佈之香港審閱
Engagements 2410 “Review of Interim Financial Information Performed by the           委 聘 準 則 第 2410 號「實 體 之 獨 立 核 數 師 審 閱 中
Independent Auditor of the Entity” issued by the Hong Kong Institute of              期財務資料」進行審閱。該等簡明綜合財務報表
Certified Public Accountants. A review of these condensed consolidated               之審 閱包 括詢問（主要 對負責財 務及 會計事 務
financial statements consists of making inquiries, primarily of persons              之人士），以及應用分析及其他審閱程序。審閱
responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying analytical and        之範圍遠較根據香港審核準則進行審核之範圍
other review procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than an audit       為小，故本核數師無法確保本核數師已知悉可
conducted in accordance with Hong Kong Standards on Auditing and                     通過審核確認之所有重要事項。因此，本核數
consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we would become             師不會發表審核意見。
aware of all significant matters that might be identified in an audit. Accordingly
we do not express an audit opinion.

PURCHASE, SALE OR REDEMPTION OF THE COMPANY’S LISTED                             購入、出售或贖回本公司之上市證
SECURITIES                                                                       券
During the Current Period, the Board considered that repurchases of Shares       於本期內，董事會認為購回股份可提高每股股
would lead to an enhancement of the earnings per Share and overall               份之盈利價值及整體股東回報，因此本公司於
shareholders return, thus the Company has purchased a total of 98,330,000        聯交所購入合共 98,330,000 股股份，總代價約為
Shares of the Company on the Stock Exchange at an aggregate consideration        港 幣 298.46 百 萬 元（不 包 括 開 支）。67,732,000 股
of approximately HK$298.46 million (excluding expenses). 67,732,000 Shares       股 份 已 於 2024 年 6 月 28 日 被 註 銷 及 30,598,000 股
were cancelled as of 28 June 2024 and 30,598,000 Shares were cancelled as of     股 份 已 於 2024 年 7 月 11 日 被 註 銷。於 2024 年 6 月

RISK MANAGEMENT                                                                    風險管理
The Board acknowledges that risk management is one of the key controls to          董事會認為風險管理是監察本集團的財務匯報
monitor the effectiveness of financial reporting and internal control systems      及內部監控系統成效的關鍵措施之一。為加強
within the Group. To enhance better corporate governance in these aspects,         有關方面的企業管治，因而成立了風險管理部。
Risk Management Department was established.

Risk Management Department                                                         風險管理部
The Risk Management Department was established by the Company in                   本公司於 2005 年 12 月成立風險管理部。風險管
December 2005. The key function of the Risk Management Department is to            理部的主要職能是提供獨立評估服務，以審查
provide an independent appraisal function to examine and evaluate                  及評估本公司及其附屬公司的營運、內部監控
operations, the systems of internal control and risk management as a service       及風險管理系統。風險管理部支援各管理層面，
to the Company and its subsidiaries. The Risk Management Department assists        為達致營運目的及目標致力改善下列各項：
all levels of administrations in the achievement of the organisational goals and
objectives by striving to provide a positive impact on:

(a)   efficiency and effectiveness of operating functions;                         (a)    營運職能的效率及成效；

(b)   reliability of financial reporting;                                          (b)    財務匯報的可靠性；

(c)   status of implementation and effectiveness of the internal control           (c)    內部監控政策及程序的實施情況及其有效
      policies and procedures; and                                                        性；及

(d)   compliance with applicable laws and regulations.                             (d)    遵守適用之法律及法規。

INTERNAL CONTROLS                                                                 內部監控
The Board acknowledges its responsibility to ensure that a sound and effective    董事會確認其有責任保證內部監控系統運作穩
internal control system is maintained. The system includes a defined              健妥善而且有效，該系統包括一個權責分明的
management structure with specified limits of authority, to:                      管理架構，旨在：

(a)     achieve business objectives and safeguard assets against unauthorised     (a)   達成業務目標及保護資產免遭非法佔用或
        use or disposition;                                                             挪用；

(b)     ensure maintenance of proper accounting records for the provision of      (b)   確保妥善存置會計記錄以備提供可靠的財
        reliable financial information for internal use or for publication; and         務資料以供內部使用或公佈用途；及

(c)     ensure compliance with the relevant legislation and regulations.          (c)   確保遵守有關法例及規定。

The internal control system is designed to provide reasonable, but not            內部監控系統旨在提供合理（而非絕對）的保證，
absolute, assurance against material misstatement or loss, and manage,            以防出現嚴重誤報或損失的情況，並管理（而非
rather than eliminate, risks of failure in operational systems, and to ensure     完全杜絕）營運系統故障的風險，以及確保達致
achievement of the Group’s objectives.                                            本集團的目標。

Internal Audit Department                                                         內部審計部
The Internal Audit Department was established since 1996, its principal duties    內部審計部於 1996 年成立，其主要職能是審查
are examining and evaluating the business operations of the sales offices and     和評估所有產業的營銷辦事處及分公司之營運
branches of all the business units and ensuring the compliance status of the      情況，以及確保本集團各產業的合規狀況。此
Group’s business units. Besides, the Internal Audit Department also carries out   外，內部審計部亦對高級職員離職（無論是辭職
special audits when senior staff leaving their positions either due to            或是本集團內部崗位調遷）進行專項審計。
resignations or job rotations within the Group.

During the Current Period, the Internal Audit Department carried out the audit    於本期，內部審計部曾進行部份主要產業的審
works of certain major business units, and provided recommendations on            核工作，並提供有效提升管理及營運效能的建議。
management enhancement and operational efficiency.
